11:T20312,<h1 id="vantage-crmp-technical-specification-design-tsd" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-3xl mt-2 mb-4">Vantage CRMP — Technical Specification Design (TSD)</h1>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>Document ID:</strong> CRMP-TSD-001  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>Version:</strong> 1.5  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>Status:</strong> Prototype / living spec  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>Products in scope:</strong> CFD + Crypto Exchange  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>Primary stack:</strong> Next.js 15 (App Router), React 19, SQLite (<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">better-sqlite3</code>), RBAC session auth  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>Owner:</strong> YAN Haixiang  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>Companion:</strong> <a class="underline" href="/admin/docs/prd">PRD</a> · <a class="underline" href="/admin/docs/user-guide">User Guide</a> · <a class="underline" href="/admin/docs/uat">UAT</a></p>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">This TSD describes the technical design of the Centralised Risk Management Platform (CRMP) Admin Control Plane.  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>§8 AI Admin</strong> and <strong>§9 Second-AI Challenger</strong> are first-class module specifications.</p>

<hr class="my-6 border-[var(--line)]" />

<h2 id="1-purpose-scope" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">1. Purpose &amp; scope</h2>

<h3 id="1-1-purpose" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">1.1 Purpose</h3>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Provide a single admin control plane where Risk, Ops, AI, and System operators can:</p>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li>Observe Monitor 2.0 indicators / alerts</li>
<li>Run AI RCA (skills + RAG) with independent second-AI challenge on high severity</li>
<li>Triage in Demo Messenger (evidence, chat, escalate, dismiss, close, controls)</li>
<li>Enforce human gates on high-impact actions</li>
<li>Govern AI configuration via maker/checker</li>
<li>Review spine logs, risk analytics, market intel, and daily performance</li>
</ul>

<h3 id="1-2-in-scope-prototype" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">1.2 In scope (prototype)</h3>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li>Admin UI + SQLite persistence</li>
<li>Detectors → Alarm → AI RCA → Second opinion → Messenger / Intervention → Spine → Dashboard</li>
<li>AI Admin governance (parameters, skills, RAG, training, accuracy)</li>
<li>Risk scenario playbooks and linked timeline chains</li>
<li>Demo Messenger + Lark channel registry (mock webhooks)</li>
<li>Market Intelligence 5-minute scanner + outbox</li>
<li>Bilingual docs (EN / zh-Hant) and responsive admin shell</li>
</ul>

<h3 id="1-3-out-of-scope-production-wiring" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">1.3 Out of scope (production wiring)</h3>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li>Live SSO / IdP</li>
<li>Real Lark interactive cards / oneZero / wallet write adapters</li>
<li>Production LLM billing + training cluster</li>
</ul>

<hr class="my-6 border-[var(--line)]" />

<h2 id="2-system-context" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">2. System context</h2>

outbox</text></g></svg></div>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>AI Admin</strong> sits beside the runtime spine: it does <strong>not</strong> execute live trading actions; it governs models, playbooks, RAG corpus, and AI parameters under dual control.</p>

<hr class="my-6 border-[var(--line)]" />

<h3 id="3-1-runtime-spine-stages" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">3.1 Runtime spine stages</h3>
<ol class="list-decimal pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><strong>Detectors</strong> sample indicators (<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/detectors</code>)</li>
<li><strong>Alarm</strong> opens Monitor alert/ticket</li>
<li><strong>AI RCA</strong> matches skill or RAG-reasons (<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/ai-analyses</code>)</li>
<li><strong>Second-AI Challenger</strong> runs when severity ≥ threshold (<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">crmp-challenger-v0</code>)</li>
<li><strong>Demo Messenger / Human intervention</strong> triage and gated controls</li>
<li><strong>Spine logging</strong> records stage transitions (<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/spine</code>)</li>
</ol>

audit</text></g></svg></div>
<ol class="list-decimal pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><strong>Daily performance / Risk Log / Market Intel</strong> aggregate outcomes</li>
</ol>

<hr class="my-6 border-[var(--line)]" />

<h2 id="4-data-model-core" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">4. Data model (core)</h2>

<h3 id="4-1-identity-rbac" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">4.1 Identity &amp; RBAC</h3>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">users</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">roles</code> (permission JSON), <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">departments</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">teams</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">sessions</code></li>
<li>Permissions are string codes; <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">SUPER_ADMIN</code> has <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">*</code></li>
</ul>

<h3 id="4-2-monitoring" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">4.2 Monitoring</h3>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">monitor_indicators</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">monitor_alerts</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">monitor_tickets</code></li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">risk_domains</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">escalation_routes</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">lark_channels</code></li>
</ul>

<h3 id="4-3-ai-runtime" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">4.3 AI runtime</h3>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_skills</code> (+ <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">scenario_json</code>), <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">risk_scenario_chains</code></li>

</ul>

<h2 id="5-security-rbac-summary" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">5. Security &amp; RBAC (summary)</h2>

<div class="overflow-x-auto my-3"><table class="w-full text-sm border-collapse">
<thead><tr>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">Concern</th>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">Rule</th>
</tr></thead><tbody>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Page access</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">getCurrentUser()</code> + <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">hasPermission(role, code)</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Maker ≠ Checker</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Proposer cannot approve own <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_change_requests</code> when <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai.maker_checker_required=true</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">AI Admin mutations</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Only via <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/api/ai-admin</code> with propose/approve permissions</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Audit</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">writeAudit</code> on propose, decide, feedback, training queue</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><strong>AI access blocklist</strong></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Pages/functions/fields/data AI must never touch — see <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/security/ai-access</code> and <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">lib/security/ai-access-blocklist.ts</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody></table></div>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Detailed AI Admin permission matrix: <strong>§8.3</strong>.  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>Human-only surfaces (identity, secrets, dual-control approve, LP/wallet execute, role writes):</strong> living list on <strong><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/security/ai-access</code></strong>.</p>

<h2 id="7-admin-surface-map" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">7. Admin surface map</h2>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Source of truth for routes: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">NAV_ITEMS</code> + <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">NAV_GROUPS</code> in <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">platform/src/lib/nav.ts</code>. Every row is specified in this TSD (this section + §8–§17) and has an operator how-to in the User Guide.</p>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Unread formula: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">max(0, mergeNavTotals(server) + extra − seen)</code>. Opening a href writes seen. <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">bumpNavBadge(href)</code> increments extra. Pages uses <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">FALLBACK_NAV_TOTALS</code> when SQLite counts are empty.</p>

Client detects <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">isPublicSnapshot()</code> / <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">NEXT_PUBLIC_STATIC_EXPORT</code> and uses demo fallbacks instead of <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/api</code>.</p>

<hr class="my-6 border-[var(--line)]" />

<h2 id="8-ai-admin-management-page-full-specification" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">8. AI Admin Management Page — full specification</h2>

<h3 id="8-1-purpose" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.1 Purpose</h3>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/ai-admin</code> is the <strong>AI control plane</strong>. Operators use it to:</p>
<ol class="list-decimal pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li>Observe AI health KPIs and accuracy history</li>
<li>Propose changes to AI parameters, skills, and RAG documents</li>
<li>Approve/reject those changes under <strong>maker/checker dual control</strong></li>
<li>Queue training / recalibration runs</li>
<li>Label RCA quality feedback (CORRECT / INCORRECT / PARTIAL)</li>
</ol>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">It is <strong>governance</strong>, not the live RCA workbench (<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/ai-analyses</code>) and not the intervention desk (<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/interventions</code>).</p>

<h3 id="8-2-route-components" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.2 Route &amp; components</h3>

<div class="overflow-x-auto my-3"><table class="w-full text-sm border-collapse">
<thead><tr>

<h3 id="8-3-access-control" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.3 Access control</h3>

<h4 id="8-3-1-page-view-any-of" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.3.1 Page view (any of)</h4>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai.admin</code></li>

</ul>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Unauthorized users redirect to <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin</code>.</p>

<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Yes*</td>

<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Primary checker; may also propose</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">RISK_ANALYST</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Yes</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Yes</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">No</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Propose + operate feedback</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">SUPER_ADMIN</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Yes</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Yes</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Yes</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Full; still blocked from self-approve when maker≠checker enforced</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">OPS_LEAD</code> / <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">SYSTEM_ADMIN</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Limited / via perms</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Per ROLE_DEFS</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Per ROLE_DEFS</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">See <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ROLE_DEFS</code> in <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">db.ts</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">VIEWER</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">No (unless granted)</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">No</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">No</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Read-only elsewhere</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table></div>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">\* Risk Owner has both propose and approve; self-approval of own CR is still rejected.</p>

<h4 id="8-3-5-maker-checker-rule" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.3.5 Maker ≠ Checker rule</h4>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">When <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">platform_settings.ai.maker_checker_required != "false"</code>:</p>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">decideChangeRequest</code> throws if <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">proposed_by === decided_by</code></li>
<li>UI should only show Approve/Reject to checkers who are not the proposer</li>
</ul>

<h3 id="8-4-data-model" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.4 Data model</h3>

<h4 id="8-4-1-ai-change-requests" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.4.1 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_change_requests</code></h4>

<h4 id="8-4-2-ai-training-runs" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.4.2 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_training_runs</code></h4>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Tracks queued/running/completed recalibrations: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">run_id</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">model_name</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">dataset_label</code>, metrics (<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">accuracy</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">precision_score</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">recall_score</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">f1_score</code>), <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">samples</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">status</code>, timestamps, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">created_by</code>.</p>

<h4 id="8-4-3-ai-feedback" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.4.3 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_feedback</code></h4>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Per-analysis labels: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">CORRECT</code> \| <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">INCORRECT</code> \| <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">PARTIAL</code>, optional note, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">rated_by</code>.</p>

<h4 id="8-4-4-ai-accuracy-snapshots" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.4.4 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_accuracy_snapshots</code></h4>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Daily rollup: skill-match rate, human-agree rate, feedback-correct rate, analyses total, intervention approved/rejected counts. Overview refresh upserts <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">date('now')</code> live snapshot.</p>

<h3 id="8-5-ui-specification-tabs" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.5 UI specification (tabs)</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Console tabs (client state):</p>

<div class="overflow-x-auto my-3"><table class="w-full text-sm border-collapse">
<thead><tr>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">Tab ID</th>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">Label</th>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">Functions</th>
</tr></thead><tbody>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">overview</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Overview</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">KPI cards: analyses total, skill-match %, avg confidence, needs-human, interventions pending, human-agree %, feedback-correct %, pending CRs. Recent analyses list with feedback labels.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">params</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Parameters</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Editable drafts for AI settings; <strong>Propose</strong> creates PARAM CR (does not apply immediately).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">changes</code></td>

<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">List CRs (PENDING first). Checker Approve / Reject with note. Shows maker name, before/after payload. Pending count badge on tab.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">skills</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Skills</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">List skills; form to <strong>propose CREATE</strong> skill (code, name, description, indicator patterns, owner dept, human step). Actual apply on approve. Link out to <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/skills</code> for scenario board.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">rag</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">RAG</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">List documents (excerpt); form to <strong>propose CREATE</strong> RAG doc; apply on approve (+ FTS reindex).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">training</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Training</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">List runs + metrics; form to <strong>queue training</strong> (creates TRAINING CR).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">history</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">History &amp; accuracy</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Accuracy snapshot table (14 days).</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table></div>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Header badges show current <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">role_code</code>, <strong>Maker</strong>, and/or <strong>Checker</strong> capability.</p>

<h3 id="8-6-api-specification" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.6 API specification</h3>

<h4 id="8-6-1-get-api-ai-admin" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.6.1 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">GET /api/ai-admin</code></h4>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Query:</p>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">tab</code> = <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">overview</code> (default) \| <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">params</code> \| <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">changes</code> \| <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">training</code> \| <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">skills</code> \| <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">rag</code></li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">status</code> (optional, for changes filter)</li>
</ul>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Auth: view permissions (§8.3.1).  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Default response includes <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">{ overview, params, changes, training, skills, rag, roles }</code>.</p>

<h4 id="8-6-2-post-api-ai-admin" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.6.2 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">POST /api/ai-admin</code></h4>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">JSON body with <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">action</code>:</p>

<div class="overflow-x-auto my-3"><table class="w-full text-sm border-collapse">
<thead><tr>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">action</code></th>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">Permission</th>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">Effect</th>
</tr></thead><tbody>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">propose</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Maker</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Generic CR insert</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">propose_param</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Maker</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">PARAM UPDATE CR for <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">key</code>/<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">value</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>

<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">SKILL CREATE/UPDATE/… CR</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">propose_rag</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">rag.manage</code> or <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai.propose</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">RAG CREATE/UPDATE/… CR</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">decide</code></td>

<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">APPROVED</code> applies payload; <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">REJECTED</code> closes CR. Enforces maker≠checker</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">queue_training</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Maker</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">TRAINING CREATE CR</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">feedback</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai.operate</code> or <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai.admin</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Insert <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_feedback</code> row</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table></div>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Error model: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">{ error: string }</code> with HTTP 400/403.</p>

<h3 id="8-7-apply-semantics-on-approved" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.7 Apply semantics (on APPROVED)</h3>

<div class="overflow-x-auto my-3"><table class="w-full text-sm border-collapse">

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Unsupported pairs throw and leave CR undecided (transactional expectation for production: wrap apply+status update).</p>

<h3 id="8-9-non-functional-requirements" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.9 Non-functional requirements</h3>
<div class="overflow-x-auto my-3"><table class="w-full text-sm border-collapse">
<thead><tr>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">NFR</th>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">Spec</th>
</tr></thead><tbody>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Latency</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Page SSR + client tabs; API mutations &lt; 500ms p95 in prototype</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Durability</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">SQLite WAL; audit trail retained</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Safety</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">No direct apply of PARAM/SKILL/RAG without CR when maker/checker required</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Separation</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Runtime interventions remain on <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/interventions</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Observability</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Overview KPIs + accuracy snapshots</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table></div>

<h3 id="8-10-ux-acceptance-criteria" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.10 UX / acceptance criteria</h3>
<ol class="list-decimal pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li>AI Engineer can open AI Admin and propose a skill; CR appears PENDING.</li>
<li>Same AI Engineer <strong>cannot</strong> approve that CR (error: maker/checker).</li>
<li>Risk Owner can approve; skill becomes ACTIVE and visible on <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/skills</code>.</li>
<li>Parameter propose does not change live value until APPROVED.</li>
<li>Training queue creates CR then TRAINING run on approve.</li>
<li>Overview shows pending CR count and accuracy history.</li>
<li>Feedback labels persist and affect feedback-correct rate.</li>
</ol>

<h3 id="8-11-sequence-propose-skill-happy-path" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.11 Sequence — propose skill (happy path)</h3>

<hr class="my-6 border-[var(--line)]" />

<h2 id="9-independent-second-ai-challenger" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">9. Independent Second-AI Challenger</h2>

<h3 id="9-1-purpose" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">9.1 Purpose</h3>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">For alert severities at or above <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai.second_opinion_severity</code> (default <strong>BREACH</strong>), the platform runs an independent challenger model (<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">crmp-challenger-v0</code>) after the primary skill/RAG RCA. The challenger <strong>must not</strong> reuse the primary decision path (separate heuristics / future separate vendor).</p>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>Code:</strong> <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">platform/src/lib/ai/challenger.ts</code> · wired from <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">analyze.ts</code> after skill/RAG persist.</p>

<h3 id="9-2-trigger" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">9.2 Trigger</h3>
<div class="overflow-x-auto my-3"><table class="w-full text-sm border-collapse">
<thead><tr>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">Setting</th>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">Default</th>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">Behaviour</th>
</tr></thead><tbody>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai.second_opinion_severity</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">BREACH</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Run when alert severity rank ≥ setting (<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">WARN</code> &amp;lt; <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">BREACH</code> &amp;lt; <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">CRITICAL</code>)</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table></div>

<div class="doc-diagram"><svg viewBox="0 0 370 480" width="370" height="480" role="img" aria-label="Flowchart"><defs><marker id="doc-arrow-21" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="9" refY="5" markerWidth="7" <h3 id="9-3-outputs" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">9.3 Outputs</h3>
<div class="overflow-x-auto my-3"><table class="w-full text-sm border-collapse">
<thead><tr>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">Field</th>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">Description</th>
</tr></thead><tbody>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">verdict</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">AGREE</code> / <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">PARTIAL</code> / <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">DISAGREE</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">critiques</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Material gaps in primary narrative</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">improvements</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Prioritised recommendations (<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">HYPOTHESIS</code> / <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">EVIDENCE</code> / <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ACTION</code> / …)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">alternatives</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Competing hypotheses with confidence</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table></div>

<h3 id="9-4-persistence-side-effects" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">9.4 Persistence &amp; side-effects</h3>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li>Table <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_analysis_challenges</code> (1:1 with <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_analyses</code>)</li>
<li>Evidence row type <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">CHALLENGER</code></li>
<li>Columns <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_analyses.challenged</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">challenge_verdict</code></li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">PARTIAL</code> / <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">DISAGREE</code> forces <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">needs_human = 1</code></li>
<li>Spine stage <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">AI_RCA</code> event + audit <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">AI_SECOND_OPINION</code></li>
<li>Lazy backfill via <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">getAnalysisBundle</code> / <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">POST /api/ai</code> action <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">backfill_challenges</code></li>
</ul>

<h3 id="9-5-ui" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">9.5 UI</h3>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li>List badges: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">2nd AI · {verdict}</code></li>
<li>Detail panel: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">AiChallengePanel</code></li>
<li>Controls: simulate CRITICAL, backfill challenges</li>
</ul>

<h2 id="10-skills-risk-scenarios-summary" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">10. Skills &amp; risk scenarios (summary)</h2>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Skills store rich <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">scenario_json</code>: indicator, thresholds + why, fault areas, escalation, BU corrections, past cases.  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">risk_scenario_chains</code> link multi-indicator timelines. See <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/skills</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">risk-scenarios-catalog.ts</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">risk-scenarios-extra.ts</code>.</p>

<h2 id="11-demo-messenger" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">11. Demo Messenger</h2>

<h3 id="11-1-purpose" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">11.1 Purpose</h3>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">In-app Lark-style inbox for alert + AI report threads with inline operator actions. Production transport remains Lark; this module proves UX and audit semantics.</p>

<h3 id="11-2-stack" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">11.2 Stack</h3>
<div class="overflow-x-auto my-3"><table class="w-full text-sm border-collapse">

<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">Path</th>
</tr></thead><tbody>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Page</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/messenger</code> → <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">app/admin/messenger/page.tsx</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">UI</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">components/DemoMessenger.tsx</code> (mobile master-detail)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">API</td>

<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Domain</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">lib/messenger/demo.ts</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody></table></div>

<h3 id="11-3-actions" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">11.3 Actions</h3>
<div class="overflow-x-auto my-3"><table class="w-full text-sm border-collapse">
<thead><tr>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">Action</th>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">Effect</th>
</tr></thead><tbody>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">show_evidence</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Post vault + challenger summary into thread</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">chat</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">User note / challenge; disagreement flags <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">needs_human</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">escalate</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Advance escalation path step</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">dismiss</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">False alarm → thread DISMISSED, alert CLOSED</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">close</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Accept AI → thread CLOSED</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">recommend</code> → <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">confirm_action</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Double-confirm control → admin_ref (+ checker if required)</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table></div>

<h3 id="11-4-data" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">11.4 Data</h3>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">messenger_threads</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">messenger_messages</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">messenger_pending_actions</code> (see §4.4).</p>

<hr class="my-6 border-[var(--line)]" />

<h2 id="12-market-intelligence" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">12. Market Intelligence</h2>

<h3 id="12-1-purpose" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">12.1 Purpose</h3>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Five-minute scan of news/social/official signals that can move LP prices; push formatted cards to a dedicated messenger/outbox channel; expose indicator <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">M2-MKT-INTEL</code>.</p>

<h3 id="12-2-key-modules" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">12.2 Key modules</h3>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">lib/market-intel/scanner.ts</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">format.ts</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">schema.ts</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">demo-scan.ts</code></li>
<li>UI <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/market-intel</code></li>
<li>Settings: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">market_intel.enabled</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">interval_minutes</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">lark_chat_id</code></li>
</ul>

<h3 id="12-3-public-snapshot-github-pages" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">12.3 Public snapshot (GitHub Pages)</h3>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Pages has no Next.js API routes. <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">POST /api/market-intel</code> would return <strong>405</strong>. The desk therefore:</p>
<ol class="list-decimal pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li>Detects <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">github.io</code> / <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/PRD/crmp-admin</code> / <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">NEXT_PUBLIC_STATIC_EXPORT</code>.</li>
<li>Runs <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">runClientMarketIntelScan()</code> from the same <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">EVENT_TEMPLATES</code> as the live scanner.</li>
<li>Updates Findings, outbox, scan log and <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">M2-MKT-INTEL</code> in local state (persisted in <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">localStorage</code>).</li>
<li>Seeds three findings at SSG time so the first paint is not empty.</li>
</ol>

<hr class="my-6 border-[var(--line)]" />

<h2 id="15-deployment-prototype" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">15. Deployment (prototype)</h2>

<pre class="my-3 overflow-x-auto rounded-lg bg-slate-900 text-slate-100 p-3 text-xs"><code>
cd platform

npm install

npm run dev   # http://localhost:3000

</code></pre>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">SQLite path: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">platform/data/vantage_risk.db</code>.  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Reset: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">npm run db:reset</code> then restart.</p>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Demo logins: see User Guide §1 (e.g. <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">admin@vantagemarkets.com</code> / <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">admin123</code>).</p>

<hr class="my-6 border-[var(--line)]" />

<h2 id="16-remaining-admin-modules" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">16. Remaining admin modules</h2>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Modules not fully specified in §8–§13. Behaviour must match the User Guide how-to and PRD §6.4.</p>

<h3 id="16-1-login-session-shell-unread-7-1" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.1 Login, session, shell, unread — §7.1</h3>

<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li>Personas: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">DEMO_PERSONAS</code> in <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">lib/demo-session.ts</code> (YAN Haixiang first).  </li>
<li>Localhost: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">POST /api/auth/login</code> sets <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">crmp_session</code> <strong>and</strong> writes demo session.  </li>
<li>Pages / 404/405: skip API, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">writeDemoSession</code> only.  </li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">AdminShell</code> prefers demo session when <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">isPublicSnapshot()</code>. Sign out clears both.  </li>
<li>Nav groups from <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">NAV_GROUPS</code>. Unread: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">AdminShell</code> + <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">bumpNavBadge</code> from Market Intel, Detectors, AI Analyses, Messenger.</li>
</ul>

<h3 id="16-2-admin-home" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.2 Admin Home</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">SSR counts (users, teams, sources, domains, open alerts/tickets, Lark channels, routes). Every tile is a <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">Link</code>: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">StatCard</code> <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">href</code> (with icon + Open), owner → <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/login</code>, messenger hero → <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/messenger</code>, jump grid, department cards to working pages (alerts / interventions / AI analyses / settings), recent alerts to <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/alerts#{alert_id}</code>, spine steps to monitor / alerts / escalation / AI / dashboard. <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">AlertsBoard</code> honours the hash.</p>

<h3 id="16-3-daily-performance" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.3 Daily performance</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">getDailyDashboard()</code> → CFD/crypto metric arrays + WARN/BREACH summary. <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">POST /api/dashboard</code> refresh.</p>

<h3 id="16-4-risk-log" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.4 Risk log</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">getRiskLogDashboard()</code> aggregates: summary USD/times, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">by_category</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">by_domain</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">loopholes</code>, chronological <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">records</code>. Read-only UI (<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">RiskLogDashboard</code>).</p>

<h3 id="16-5-monitor-2-0-hub" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.5 Monitor 2.0 hub</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Query <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">tab=indicators|alerts|tickets</code>. <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">MonitorActions</code> <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">sync_monitor2</code> / <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ack_alert</code> / <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">update_ticket</code>. Setting <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">monitor2.base_url</code> displayed.</p>

<h3 id="16-6-detectors" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.6 Detectors</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Table <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">detectors</code> + <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">detector_runs</code>. <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">POST /api/detectors</code> <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">{ raiseAlarms: true }</code> or <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">{ action: 'toggle' }</code>. Non-HEALTHY results bump nav badges for detectors/alerts/ai-analyses.</p>

<h3 id="16-7-live-alerts" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.7 Live alerts</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Join <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">monitor_alerts</code> × indicators. Sort CRITICAL/BREACH/WARN then time. <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ack_alert</code> when <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">monitor.operate</code>.</p>

<h3 id="16-8-risk-domains" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.8 Risk domains</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">risk_domains</code> cards: priority, product_coverage, owner_department, supporting_departments_json. Read-only.</p>

<h3 id="16-9-ai-analyses-list-detail" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.9 AI Analyses list/detail</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">List: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">AiAnalysesBoard</code> simulate actions <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">simulate_copy_breach</code>, EQ drawdown, CRITICAL, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">backfill_challenges</code>. Detail: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/ai-analyses/[id]</code> evidence + <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">AiChallengePanel</code>. See §9.</p>

<h3 id="16-10-skills-board-skill-md-page" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.10 Skills board + SKILL.md page</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">SkillsScenariosBoard</code>: search, skills vs chains tabs, <strong>Enter</strong> → <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/skills/[code]</code> (<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">finalizeSkill</code> playbook: when to use/not, prechecks, steps, evidence, stop, success). Catalog: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">risk-scenarios-catalog.ts</code> + extras.</p>

<h3 id="16-11-knowledge-tree" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.11 Knowledge Tree</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">KnowledgeTreeBoard</code> client SVG (<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">viewBox</code> width 1120). Trunks: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">domains</code> | <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">chains</code> | <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">rag</code>. Product filter ALL/CFD/Crypto. Domain nodes wrap (5-col × 2). Click domain fans skills; click skill fills inspector; <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">router.push</code> playbook (do not use invalid SVG <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">&lt;Link&gt;</code>). RAG docs scored from <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">tags_json</code> + title. Outline mode is the same graph as a nested list.</p>

<h3 id="16-12-rag-corpus" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.12 RAG corpus</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">RagManager</code>: category filter, search, retrieve <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">GET /api/rag?mode=retrieve&amp;q=&amp;limit=6</code>, create/update/retire when <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">rag.manage</code>. FTS via <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">reindexRagFts</code>. Governed creates should prefer AI Admin CR path (§8.7).</p>

<h3 id="16-13-spine-log" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.13 Spine log</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Stages: DETECT, ALARM, AI_RCA, SKILL_EXECUTE, HUMAN_INTERVENTION, RESOLVED, DASHBOARD. 24h <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">StatCard</code> counts + last 150 events.</p>

<h3 id="16-14-interventions" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.14 Interventions</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">listInterventions()</code>. Pending: note + Approve/Reject via <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">decideInterventionAction</code>. Writes spine + audit. Distinct from AI Admin CRs.</p>

<h3 id="16-15-lark-escalation" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.15 Lark + escalation</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">lark_channels</code> + <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">lark.*</code> settings. <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">escalation_routes</code> join teams + channel. Messenger <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">escalate</code> walks primary → secondary → owner → exec using this map.</p>

<h3 id="16-16-organisation" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.16 Organisation</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Departments (responsibilities JSON), teams (Lark chat, on-call, member_count), roles (<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">permissions_json</code> chips), users (<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">UsersManager</code> create/toggle when <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">users.manage</code>). Seed includes <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">PLATFORM_OWNER</code>.</p>

<h3 id="16-17-data-sources" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.17 Data sources</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">data_sources</code> registry. <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">DataSourcesManager</code> CRUD when <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">sources.manage</code>.</p>

<h3 id="16-18-ai-access-blocklist" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.18 AI access blocklist</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">lib/security/ai-access-blocklist.ts</code> → <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">AI_ACCESS_BLOCKLIST</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">AI_ALLOWED_CAPABILITIES</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">AI_SERVICE_ROLE_FORBIDDEN_PERMISSIONS</code>. Read-only board + stats.</p>

<h3 id="16-19-audit-log" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.19 Audit log</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">audit_logs</code> ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 200. Actor, action, entity, details_json.</p>

<h3 id="16-20-platform-settings" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.20 Platform settings</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">SettingsManager</code> groups by key prefix: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">platform.|products.</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">monitor2.</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai.</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">market_intel.</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">lark.</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">escalation.|detectors.</code>. <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">PATCH /api/settings</code>. Pages: message “stored in this browser only”.</p>

<h3 id="16-21-docs-renderer" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">16.21 Docs renderer</h3>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">Markdown <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">platform/docs/*.md</code> + <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">*.zh-Hant.md</code>. <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">markdownToHtml</code>: headings h1–h4, tables, lists, mermaid <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">graph</code> / <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">flowchart</code> / <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">sequenceDiagram</code> → SVG (<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">.doc-diagram</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">lib/docs-mermaid.ts</code>). UAT: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">UatChecklistBoard</code> + <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">UAT_CASES</code> (45). URL catalog: <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">lib/docs/urls.ts</code> <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">PLATFORM_URLS</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">PUBLIC_ADMIN_URL</code>, <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">PUBLIC_MESSENGER_URL</code>.</p>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>Companion:</strong> <a class="underline" href="./TSD.zh-Hant.md">繁體中文版 TSD</a> · rendered at <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/docs/tsd</code></p>
12:T1f7cf,<h1 id="vantage-crmp-技術規格設計-tsd" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-3xl mt-2 mb-4">Vantage CRMP — 技術規格設計（TSD）</h1>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>文件編號：</strong> CRMP-TSD-001  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>版本：</strong> 1.5  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>狀態：</strong> 原型／持續更新  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>產品範圍：</strong> CFD + 加密貨幣交易所  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>主要技術棧：</strong> Next.js 15（App Router）、React 19、SQLite（<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">better-sqlite3</code>）、RBAC Session 驗證  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>負責人：</strong> YAN Haixiang  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>相關文件：</strong> <a class="underline" href="/admin/docs/prd">PRD</a> · <a class="underline" href="/admin/docs/user-guide">使用手冊</a> · <a class="underline" href="/admin/docs/uat">UAT</a></p>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">本 TSD 描述中央風險管理平台（CRMP）管理控制平面之技術設計。  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>§8 AI Admin</strong> 與 <strong>§9 第二 AI 挑戰者</strong>為一級模組規格。</p>

<hr class="my-6 border-[var(--line)]" />

<h2 id="1-目的與範圍" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">1. 目的與範圍</h2>

<h3 id="1-1-目的" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">1.1 目的</h3>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">提供單一管理控制平面，讓風險、營運、AI、系統人員可以：</p>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li>監看 Monitor 2.0 指標／警報</li>
<li>執行 AI 根因分析（Skills + RAG），並於高嚴重度執行獨立第二 AI 挑戰</li>
<li>於 Demo Messenger 分流（證據、聊天、升級、排除、結案、控制）</li>
<li>對高影響動作強制人工關卡</li>
<li>以 Maker/Checker 治理 AI 設定</li>
<li>檢視 Spine、風險分析、市場情報與每日績效</li>
</ul>

<h3 id="1-2-原型範圍內" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">1.2 原型範圍內</h3>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li>管理 UI + SQLite 持久化</li>
<li>Detectors → Alarm → AI RCA → 第二意見 → Messenger／Intervention → Spine → Dashboard</li>
<li>AI Admin 治理（參數、Skills、RAG、訓練、準確率）</li>
<li>風險情境劇本與多指標時間鏈</li>
<li>Demo Messenger + Lark 頻道登錄（模擬 Webhook）</li>
<li>市場情報 5 分鐘掃描與 outbox</li>
<li>雙語文件（英／繁中）與響應式管理殼層</li>
</ul>

<h3 id="1-3-原型範圍外-正式接線" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">1.3 原型範圍外（正式接線）</h3>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li>正式 SSO／IdP</li>
<li>真實 Lark 互動卡片／oneZero／錢包寫入適配</li>
<li>正式 LLM 計費與訓練叢集</li>
</ul>

<hr class="my-6 border-[var(--line)]" />

<h2 id="2-系統脈絡" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">2. 系統脈絡</h2>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>AI Admin</strong> 位於執行期 Spine 旁側：不直接執行交易動作；在雙人管控下治理模型、劇本、RAG 語料與 AI 參數。</p>

<hr class="my-6 border-[var(--line)]" />

<h3 id="3-1-執行期-spine-階段" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">3.1 執行期 Spine 階段</h3>
<ol class="list-decimal pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><strong>Detectors</strong> 採樣指標（<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/detectors</code>）</li>
<li><strong>Alarm</strong> 建立 Monitor 警報／工單</li>
<li><strong>AI RCA</strong> 匹配 Skill 或 RAG 推論（<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/ai-analyses</code>）</li>
<li><strong>第二 AI 挑戰者</strong> 於嚴重度達門檻時執行（<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">crmp-challenger-v0</code>）</li>
<li><strong>Demo Messenger／人工介入</strong> 分流與關卡控制</li>
<li><strong>Spine 日誌</strong> 記錄階段轉換（<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/spine</code>）</li>
</ol>

<ol class="list-decimal pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><strong>每日績效／Risk Log／市場情報</strong> 彙總結果</li>
</ol>

<hr class="my-6 border-[var(--line)]" />

<h2 id="4-資料模型-核心" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">4. 資料模型（核心）</h2>

<h3 id="4-1-身分與-rbac" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">4.1 身分與 RBAC</h3>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">users</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">roles</code>（權限 JSON）、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">departments</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">teams</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">sessions</code></li>
<li>權限為字串代碼；<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">SUPER_ADMIN</code> 擁有 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">*</code></li>
</ul>

<h3 id="4-2-監控" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">4.2 監控</h3>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">monitor_indicators</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">monitor_alerts</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">monitor_tickets</code></li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">risk_domains</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">escalation_routes</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">lark_channels</code></li>
</ul>

<h3 id="4-3-ai-執行期" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">4.3 AI 執行期</h3>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_skills</code>（含 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">scenario_json</code>）、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">risk_scenario_chains</code></li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">rag_documents</code>（含 FTS）</li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_analyses</code>（含 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">challenged</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">challenge_verdict</code>）、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_analysis_evidence</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_skill_runs</code></li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_analysis_challenges</code>（第二意見）</li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">interventions</code>、Spine 相關表</li>
</ul>

<h3 id="4-4-messenger" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">4.4 Messenger</h3>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">messenger_threads</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">messenger_messages</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">messenger_pending_actions</code></li>
</ul>

<h3 id="4-5-市場情報" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">4.5 市場情報</h3>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">market_intel_*</code> 掃描／發現／outbox 表（見 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">lib/market-intel/schema.ts</code>）</li>
</ul>

<h3 id="4-6-ai-admin-治理" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">4.6 AI Admin 治理</h3>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">見 <strong>§8.4</strong> — <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_change_requests</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_training_runs</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_feedback</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_accuracy_snapshots</code>，以及 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">platform_settings</code> 中的 AI 鍵值。</p>

<hr class="my-6 border-[var(--line)]" />

<h2 id="5-安全與-rbac-摘要" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">5. 安全與 RBAC（摘要）</h2>

<div class="overflow-x-auto my-3"><table class="w-full text-sm border-collapse">
<thead><tr>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">議題</th>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">規則</th>
</tr></thead><tbody>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">頁面存取</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">getCurrentUser()</code> + <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">hasPermission(role, code)</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Maker ≠ Checker</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">當 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai.maker_checker_required=true</code> 時，提案者不可核准自己的變更單</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">AI Admin 變更</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">僅能經 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/api/ai-admin</code> 且具備 propose／approve 權限</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">稽核</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">提案、裁決、回饋、訓練排隊皆 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">writeAudit</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><strong>AI 存取封鎖清單</strong></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">AI 不得觸及之頁面／功能／欄位／資料 — 見 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/security/ai-access</code> 與 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">lib/security/ai-access-blocklist.ts</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody></table></div>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">AI Admin 權限矩陣詳見 <strong>§8.3</strong>。  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><strong>僅限人工操作之表面（身分、密鑰、雙人核准、LP／錢包執行、角色寫入）：</strong> 動態清單見 <strong><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/security/ai-access</code></strong>。</p>

<hr class="my-6 border-[var(--line)]" />

<h2 id="7-管理介面地圖" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">7. 管理介面地圖</h2>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">路由真實來源：<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">platform/src/lib/nav.ts</code> 的 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">NAV_ITEMS</code>＋<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">NAV_GROUPS</code>。每一列都在本 TSD（本節＋§8–§16）有規格，使用手冊有操作說明。</p>

<hr class="my-6 border-[var(--line)]" />

<h2 id="8-ai-admin-管理頁-完整規格" class="font-[family-name:var(--font-display)] text-2xl mt-8 mb-3">8. AI Admin 管理頁 — 完整規格</h2>

<h3 id="8-1-目的" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.1 目的</h3>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/ai-admin</code> 為 <strong>AI 控制平面</strong>。操作人員用它來：</p>
<ol class="list-decimal pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li>監看 AI 健康 KPI 與準確率歷史</li>
<li>提案變更 AI 參數、Skills、RAG 文件</li>
<li>在 <strong>Maker/Checker 雙人管控</strong> 下核准／駁回變更</li>
<li>排隊訓練／重新校正工作</li>
<li>標註 RCA 品質回饋（CORRECT／INCORRECT／PARTIAL）</li>
</ol>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">此頁是<strong>治理</strong>，不是即時 RCA 工作台（<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/ai-analyses</code>），也不是介入櫃檯（<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/interventions</code>）。</p>

<h3 id="8-2-路由與元件" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.2 路由與元件</h3>

<div class="overflow-x-auto my-3"><table class="w-full text-sm border-collapse">
<thead><tr>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">項目</th>

</tr></thead><tbody>
<tr>

</tr>

</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">種子資料</td>

<h3 id="8-3-存取控制" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.3 存取控制</h3>

<h4 id="8-3-1-頁面檢視-符合任一即可" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.3.1 頁面檢視（符合任一即可）</h4>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai.admin</code></li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">skills.manage</code></li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">rag.manage</code></li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai.read</code></li>
</ul>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">未授權使用者重導向 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin</code>。</p>

<h4 id="8-3-2-maker-提案-符合任一即可" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.3.2 Maker（提案）— 符合任一即可</h4>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai.propose</code></li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">skills.manage</code></li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">rag.manage</code></li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">settings.manage</code></li>
</ul>

<h4 id="8-3-3-checker-核准-駁回-符合任一即可" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.3.3 Checker（核准／駁回）— 符合任一即可</h4>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai.approve</code></li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">skills.approve</code></li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">rag.approve</code></li>
</ul>

<h4 id="8-3-5-maker-checker-規則" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.3.5 Maker ≠ Checker 規則</h4>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">當 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">platform_settings.ai.maker_checker_required != "false"</code>：</p>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li>若 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">proposed_by === decided_by</code>，<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">decideChangeRequest</code> 拋錯</li>
<li>UI 僅應對非提案者之 Checker 顯示核准／駁回</li>
</ul>

<h3 id="8-4-資料模型" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.4 資料模型</h3>

<h4 id="8-4-1-ai-change-requests" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.4.1 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_change_requests</code></h4>

<h4 id="8-4-2-ai-training-runs" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.4.2 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_training_runs</code></h4>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">追蹤排隊／執行中／完成之重新校正：<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">run_id</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">model_name</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">dataset_label</code>、指標（<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">accuracy</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">precision_score</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">recall_score</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">f1_score</code>）、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">samples</code>、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">status</code>、時間戳、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">created_by</code>。</p>

<h4 id="8-4-3-ai-feedback" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.4.3 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_feedback</code></h4>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">每筆分析標籤：<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">CORRECT</code> \| <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">INCORRECT</code> \| <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">PARTIAL</code>，可附註記、<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">rated_by</code>。</p>

<h4 id="8-4-4-ai-accuracy-snapshots" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.4.4 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_accuracy_snapshots</code></h4>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">每日彙總：Skill 匹配率、人工同意率、回饋正確率、分析總數、介入核准／駁回數。Overview 重新整理時會 upsert 當日 live 快照。</p>

<h3 id="8-5-ui-規格-分頁" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.5 UI 規格（分頁）</h3>

<div class="overflow-x-auto my-3"><table class="w-full text-sm border-collapse">
<thead><tr>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">分頁 ID</th>

</tr></thead><tbody>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">overview</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Overview</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">KPI：分析總數、Skill 匹配%、平均信心、需人工、待介入、人工同意%、回饋正確%、待決 CR；近期分析與回饋</td>

<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">AI 設定草稿；<strong>Propose</strong> 建立 PARAM CR（不立即生效）</td>
</tr>

<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">CR 清單（PENDING 優先）。Checker 核准／駁回並填註記；顯示 Maker、before/after；分頁顯示待決數</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">skills</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Skills</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Skill 清單；表單<strong>提案建立</strong> Skill；核准後才套用；可連至 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/skills</code> 情境板</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">rag</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">RAG</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">文件清單（摘要）；表單提案建立；核准後套用並重建 FTS</td>
</tr>

<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">訓練執行清單與指標；表單排隊訓練（建立 TRAINING CR）</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">history</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">History &amp; accuracy</td>

</tr>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">頁首徽章顯示目前 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">role_code</code>、<strong>Maker</strong> 與／或 <strong>Checker</strong> 能力。</p>

<h3 id="8-6-api-規格" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.6 API 規格</h3>

<h4 id="8-6-1-get-api-ai-admin" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.6.1 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">GET /api/ai-admin</code></h4>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">查詢參數：</p>
<ul class="list-disc pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">tab</code> = <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">overview</code>（預設）\| <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">params</code> \| <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">changes</code> \| <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">training</code> \| <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">skills</code> \| <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">rag</code></li>
<li><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">status</code>（可選，篩選變更單）</li>
</ul>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">驗證：§8.3.1 檢視權限。  </p>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">預設回應含 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">{ overview, params, changes, training, skills, rag, roles }</code>。</p>

<h4 id="8-6-2-post-api-ai-admin" class="text-base font-semibold mt-4 mb-1">8.6.2 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">POST /api/ai-admin</code></h4>
<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">JSON <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">action</code>：</p>

<div class="overflow-x-auto my-3"><table class="w-full text-sm border-collapse">
<thead><tr>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">action</code></th>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">權限</th>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">效果</th>
</tr></thead><tbody>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">propose</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Maker</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">通用 CR 寫入</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">propose_param</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Maker</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">PARAM UPDATE CR</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">propose_skill</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">skills.manage</code> 或 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai.propose</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">SKILL CR</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">propose_rag</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">rag.manage</code> 或 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai.propose</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">RAG CR</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">decide</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Checker</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">APPROVED</code> 套用；<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">REJECTED</code> 結案；強制 Maker≠Checker</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">queue_training</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Maker</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">TRAINING CREATE CR</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">feedback</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top"><code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai.operate</code> 或 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai.admin</code></td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">寫入 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">ai_feedback</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody></table></div>

<p class="text-sm leading-relaxed my-2">錯誤：<code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">{ error: string }</code>，HTTP 400／403。</p>

<h3 id="8-9-非功能需求" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.9 非功能需求</h3>
<div class="overflow-x-auto my-3"><table class="w-full text-sm border-collapse">
<thead><tr>
<th class="border border-[var(--line)] bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 text-left">NFR</th>

</tr></thead><tbody>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">延遲</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">SSR + 客戶端分頁；原型突變 API p95 &lt; 500ms</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">耐久性</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">SQLite WAL；稽核保留</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">安全</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">啟用雙人制時 PARAM／SKILL／RAG 不可直接套用</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">分離</td>

<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">可觀測性</td>
<td class="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1.5 align-top">Overview KPI + 準確率快照</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table></div>

<h3 id="8-10-驗收標準" class="text-lg font-semibold mt-6 mb-2">8.10 驗收標準</h3>
<ol class="list-decimal pl-5 my-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
<li>AI Engineer 可開啟 AI Admin 並提案 Skill；CR 為 PENDING。</li>
<li>同一 AI Engineer <strong>不可</strong>核准該 CR（Maker/Checker 錯誤）。</li>
<li>Risk Owner 可核准；Skill 變 ACTIVE 並出現於 <code class="rounded bg-slate-100 px-1 text-[0.9em]">/admin/skills</code>。</li>
<li>參數提案在 APPROVED 前不改即時值。</li>
<li>訓練排隊先建 CR，核准後建立 TRAINING run。</li>
<li>Overview 顯示待決 CR 數與準確率歷史。</li>
<li>回饋標籤持久化並影響回饋正確率。</li>
</ol>
